{"forms":[{"form":"comparatives","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The degree of comparison of an adjective or adverb used when comparing two entities in terms of a certain property or a certain way of doing something. In English, the comparative of superiority is formed by adding the suffix -er or the word more (e.g. bigger, more fully); the comparative of equality, by adding the word as (e.g. as big, as fully); the comparative of inferiority, by adding the word less (e.g. less big, less fully)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"comparative degree"}],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["An adjective or adverb in the comparative degree."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["Data used to make a comparison."],"tags":["in-plural"]},{"glosses":["An equal; a rival; a compeer."],"tags":["obsolete"]},{"glosses":["One who makes comparisons; one who affects wit."],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/kəmˈpæɹətɪv/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"enpr":"kəmʹpăr-ə-tĭv","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/kəmˈpæɹətɪv/"},{"ipa":"/kəmˈpɛɹətɪv/"}],"upstream_line":16883,"upstream_offset":114337740,"upstream_record_sha256":"4d60f36d719bbb02d3cb064b1cde3d49423398aa744ee59816be928099007050","word":"comparative"}
